## 2026-10-25 (7): T32 S1 driver skeleton (`claude/t32-s1`; QEMU-tested only, not on a camera)

- **New `driver/t32` (`tx-isp-t32.ko`), a typed driver for the T32 (PRJ007).** Not a copy of the recovered T41 module: T32 keeps the T41 ISP core but its libimp/sensor ABI differs (sensor registration 116 bytes, framechan ioctls nr 131-140 with a 96-byte buffer, subdevice ops at 0x108, new DEFAULT_BIN/TIZIANO_PAR/MEMORY_MALLOC ioctls). Shared pieces come from `driver/common` (sensor registry with T32 offsets) and `driver/t23` (MSCA geometry rules); the T32 ABI, register map, MSCA channel layout, RCFG register-list format and the ISP working-memory size are in headers (`tx_isp_t32_abi.h`, `_regs.h`, `_msca.h`, `_rcfg.h`, `_mem.h`).
- **QEMU `-M ingenic-t32` (kernel 4.4.94, prebuilt `sensor_cv8001_t32.ko`, vendor libimp 2.2.0):** `IMP_ISP_Open`, `AddSensor`, `EnableSensor` (sensor init + stream-on, 488 sensor I2C writes), `IMP_System_Init` and `EnableTuning` return 0; the ioctl sequence and the returned values match the vendor driver's run up to the framechan `SET_FORMAT`. rmmod/modprobe of both modules and a second run: clean, no oops. `FrameSource_EnableChn` still fails (`REQBUFS` not implemented): frames, VIC/CSI/MSCA programming and tisp/3A are the next stages (S2/S3). Host test `tests/tx_isp_t32_mem_rcfg_test` (memory size equals the vendor's 0x9f9000 at 3840x2160; RCFG builder/walker).
- libimp 2.2.0 details the driver has to honour (found in the QEMU runs): `DRIVER_VERSION` takes a 64-byte buffer (SoC + version strings compared with libimp's, module parameters `driver_soc`/`driver_version`), `DEFAULT_BIN` an 880-byte buffer (IQ bin format version and SoC at 863/871), `ENUMINPUT` returns `{index, name[32]}`.

## 2026-10-25 (8): T31 builds on Linux 7.2 again: `cpu_has_mxu_v2` fallback (`claude/k72-t31-mxu`; build-verified, no device test needed)

- The MXU2 AWB code (24.10.) used `cpu_has_mxu_v2`, which only exists in the Ingenic 3.10/4.4 kernels; the 7.2 T31 build failed with "undeclared". `tx_isp_t31_awb_simd.h` now defines it to 0 when the kernel has no such macro (`#ifndef`), so on 7.x the selftest reports "no MXU2" and the C path is used.
- 3.10 unchanged: the macro exists there, so the `#ifndef` is skipped; T31 3.10 `objdump -d` before/after is identical (101753 lines, 0 diff). 7.2 T31 build completes (578 warning lines, rc=0).

## 2026-10-25 (7): Linux 7.x runtime warnings: proc re-publish and unpaired `s_stream` (`claude/k72-warn-next`; sinfo part device-tested on T31 .26, pairing part >=5.0 only and untested)

Ported from the 7.2 bring-up (`k72-warn`), only the parts that are valid on 3.10/4.4 as well. No behaviour change on a clean run.

- **`tx_isp_sinfo`**: the vendor proc files are removed (`remove_proc_subtree`, silent when absent) before `proc_create_data`, so a second publish no longer triggers "proc_dir_entry already registered".
- **`tx_isp_v4l2_sensor` (T31 bridge, kernel >= 5.0 only; not compiled on 3.10/4.4)**: the sensor's `s_stream` is tracked and kept paired (a stop without a start, or a double start, no longer reaches the sensor driver; 7.x warns about it).
- Device test (T31L .26, agg46, 3.10): test ko reloaded 2x, 5x application restart, 2x day/night, 5 min stream (10 snapshots): every snapshot 200, 0 oops/WARNING, no "already registered", stock ko restored. The s_stream pairing is >=5.0 only: device test on 7.2 pending.
- **Not ported**: the IRQ-depth change in `tx_isp_enable_irq` (it was reverted on the 7.2 branch because it kept the ISP IRQ masked, no frames).
- Compiler warnings: unchanged on T21/T23/T30/T31/T40/T41 (3.10/4.4 builds) and on the 7.2 T31 objects touched; T10/T20 cannot be built here (external/ingenic-sdk not populated).

## 2026-10-25: T30 in QEMU: six oops/lockup fixes (`claude/t30-qemu`; QEMU-tested, not yet on a camera)

First run of the recovered T30 driver on the QEMU `ingenic-t30` machine with the thingino `qemu_t30x` image (kernel 3.10.14, sc4236 model on i2c0, `sensor_sc4236_t30.ko`). Before: the image oopsed during boot, `rmmod` of the sensor panicked, the first reload hung in `modprobe`, no-sensor tuning calls oopsed. After: 10 x (register sensor, 37 tuning cases, `DelSensor`, `rmmod` both modules, `modprobe` both) without oops, lockup or "nobody cared"; 74 tuning cases without a sensor: 0 oopses. All fixes are lift errors in `tx_isp_t30_recovered.c` (plus one missing export).

- **`/proc/jz/isp/isp-m0` read oopsed** (daynightd reads it at boot): `isp_info_proc_fops`, `ncu_proc_fops` and `ldc_proc_fops` had `.read = seq_read` but no `.open`, so `seq_read` locked a NULL `seq_file` (`mutex_lock` at 0x28; the close path then oopsed in `single_release`). The three fops now have their open handlers; the lifted `tx_isp_ncu_open` / `tx_isp_ldc_open` passed a text offset as the show function and the inode as the file and were rewritten to `single_open_size(show, PDE_DATA, 2048)`.
- **`sensor_sc4236_t30` did not load** ("Unknown symbol isp_printf"): `isp_printf` was not exported (the thingino sensor modules import it, as on every other SoC). Exported, and added to `driver/isp_imports.txt` so `check_isp_exports.sh` guards it.
- **Oops in `apical_sensor_calibration_update`** (tuning control that writes a calibration table, e.g. `SetHiLightDepress`): the lifted call in `apical_api_calibration` passed 3 of the 5 arguments (length and status pointer dropped, the callee wrote its status through a stale register: "Unhandled kernel unaligned access", BadVA 0x92); and the copy loop stored `((void **)dst)[i] = src[i]` (4 bytes stride, 4x overrun of the table). Both fixed. `apical_api_calibration` also returns 5 ("not available") before the firmware instance exists (core + 0x19c) and when its per-set table pointer is not populated instead of reading `0x1526`.
- **`rmmod sensor_*` panicked** ("Fatal exception in interrupt"): the core keeps the sensor attribute pointer (core + 0x120) in the sensor module's data; the AE interrupt read it after the module was freed. The pointer is dropped in `private_i2c_del_driver` and in a module notifier (GOING, after the sensor's exit, before its memory is freed).
- **First reload after streaming hung in `modprobe`** (soft lockup): the VIC is never stopped, so a frame-done status latched by the previous load fired into the half-initialised handler, which returns without acknowledging; the level IRQ livelocked before `tx_isp_request_irq` could disable it. The line is now requested with `IRQ_NOAUTOEN` (depth 1, same as the old "request, then disable").
- **`DisableSensor`/`DelSensor` on an unknown sensor name oopsed on LIST_POISON2** (second call after a failed `AddSensor`): the name search ended on the list head and the code unlinked the head itself. Not found now returns 0 without touching the list.
- Observed, not changed: after a streamer exits without `DelSensor` the i2c client of the sensor stays and the next `AddSensor` fails with -EBUSY ("Failed to register i2c client sc4236 at 0x30 (-16)"); needs a check on a T30 camera (the first start after boot is fine). Most OpenIMP tuning controls (BcshHue, AeComp get, BacklightComp, HiLightDepress, DRC/Temper/Sinter strength, WB, flip, DPC, ...) reach the driver's `default:` branch and return -1; only contrast, sharpness, anti-flicker, max again/dgain, front crop and the getters of the denoise/DRC attributes answer.

## 2026-10-25 (7): T40 DPC, LCE and SDNS in plain C instead of the literal mips2c chains (`claude/t40-c-chains`; host bit-exact, .184 picture equal to next)

- **T40: DPC, LCE and SDNS rebuilt as readable C** (`driver/t40/tx_isp_t40_dpc.h`, `tx_isp_t40_lce.h`, `tx_isp_t40_sdns.h`, shared OEM table interpolation `tx_isp_t40_intp.h`) from the OEM flow; the literal chains `tx_isp_t40_{dpc,lce,sdns}_lit.inc` are removed, the chain state lives in `.t40c2`. SDNS: 844137 register writes compared, 0 differences. Host comparison against the literal chains (`tests/t40/c_chains_host`): 31 real tuning bins (26 SDK 1.3.1, 5 Eufy) + 8 random bins, day/night banks, gain walks, statistics frames through the LCE IRQ/event path, WDR light lock: DPC 2.8 M and LCE 17.2 M register writes plus the full state identical, 0 differences; deliberate one-character changes are detected. Module (stripped, against next 7970b5dc+layout/LCE-reset): 1502948 -> 1462616 bytes (xz 420724 -> 409444), .text -33 KB, about 36 KB less .bss (emulated stacks).
- The LCE session reset of the literal glue (OEM `tisp_lce_remove`, merged separately) is part of the C version.
- .184 picture A/B next / C chains / next (same scene, light 255/3649K, settled frames): edge 4.61 / 4.57 / 4.64, noise 5.50 / 5.47 / 5.54, HSV S 40.0 / 40.0 / 40.0, chart chroma 0.77 / 0.77 / 0.76, dE00 9.7 / 9.7 / 9.8, AE 37630/3336 in all three: equal within noise. (An earlier A/B against the agg46 stock module showed the next-vs-agg46 difference, not the chains.)

## 2026-10-25 (6): T23 `mdns_ref_hold` module parameter (`claude/t23-mdns-hold`; T23 .30 device-tested on a test ko)

- **T23: `mdns_ref_hold` (0644, default 0 = unchanged behaviour, no cost).** The MDNS reference weight words (`0x799c` luma, `0x7a6c` chroma) are recomputed on every gain step; a user of the reference plane (OpenIMP test pattern mode 5 writes a picture into it) lost 3 % of its frames to the rewrite between its own register writes. With bit 0 set the writers emit `0x00FFFFFF` (reference weight 1.0) instead of the computed word, and setting the parameter rewrites the words at once (clearing it restores the computed values). Bits 1 and 2 additionally hold the fake/mv words (`0x7998`, `0x7a68`) and the r_max/r_min words (`0x79a0`, `0x7a70`); on the device they did not help (chroma slightly worse), so they are experiments only. Device (.30, test ko by rmmod/insmod, OpenIMP mode 5): luma SSIM vs the ideal picture mean 0.967 / min 0.9666 / p1 0.9666 over 100 frames, no dips (without the hold: mean 0.943, 3 % of frames ~0.4); 5 on/off cycles clean, registers back to the computed words, 0 new oops, stock ko restored afterwards. Chroma SSIM only +0.04 over the live picture (U 0.816 / V 0.843 vs 0.778 / 0.805); the C-block median limits, BGM, SAD thresholds and corner words (`0x7a74`, `0x7a80`, `0x7a64`, `0x7a04`) were tried by register and did not purify it. Host test `tests/tx_isp_t23_mdns_hold_test`.
## 2026-10-25 (5): T40 colour after night->day fixed; DMSC follows the bank in day by default (`claude/t40-sharp1025`; .184)

- **T40: grey/green-magenta day picture after a night->day switch fixed (`t40_dn_keep_awb1`, default 1).** The sc830ai night bank bypasses AWB1 (TOP 0x40 bit 6), the day bank enables it. Re-enabling AWB1 on the running pipe left the day picture nearly grey with green/magenta hues in about half of the switches (HSV S 11 instead of 40, chart chroma 0.39 instead of 1.07), on stock as well and on the CLI `daynight` and the application's `running_mode` path alike, until a later TOP change happened to heal it. On .184 a bare devmem toggle of bit 6 reproduced it 5 of 10 times, toggles of other bits never; ISP register contents were not the cause (identical colour registers, no single block restored it), a TOP write at a frame end did not help (7/15). The driver now keeps AWB1 at its day-bank state through the night: 0 of 32 night->day switches bad (before 15 of 27), night stays monochrome, lit night luma unchanged (122.1 / 122.2). `t40_dn_keep_awb1=0` follows the night bank again.
- **T40: `t40_dmsc_day_follow` now defaults to 1.** The literal DMSC chain runs in day mode as on the vendor stack. .184 light 255/3649K, fresh load: day edge energy 4.22 -> 4.95 (+17 %), chart chroma 1.06 -> 1.05, dE00 5.7 -> 5.4; in the later scene stock 3.41-3.68 vs 4.59 (vendor/open stock ratio on 10-24 was 1.22). The 10-24 "chroma 0.38" against it was the AWB1 bug above. Both new parameters live in the `.t40c2` section.

## 2026-10-25 (4): T40 8-bit table interpolation rounds like the OEM; DMSC day follow option (`claude/t40-sharp1025`; host-tested, not yet on the device)

- **T40: `tisp_simple_intp_int8` truncated instead of rounding.** The OEM helper (1.3.1 firmware 0x16600) adds bit 15 of the product `diff * frac` (round half up); the reconstruction took bit 15 of the already shifted value, which is always 0. Every 8-bit gain/EV-interpolated parameter between two bin nodes (DMSC, YSP sharpening, SDNS, CDNS, LCE and the other lifted callers) could be one step off, towards the lower node. With the fix the literal DMSC chain at the vendor's day TotalGainDb (75639) equals the vendor day register dump of .184 bit for bit (before: 10 words off, e.g. 0xa014 0x001c0016 against 0x001e0018); the night comparison stays bit-exact (`tests/t40/dmsc_night_host`, host copy of the helper fixed too). The DPC literal copy already rounded correctly.
- **T40: `t40_dmsc_day_follow` (default 0).** With `t40_dmsc_follow=1` the literal DMSC chain then also runs in day mode, as the OEM does (the vendor day block is exactly the day bank at the total gain); the default keeps the static day block until a device proof (chroma and edge energy in light). The open live day block matches no single gain of the day bank (15+ words off at the best gain), so it is a real difference to the vendor's day sharpening.
- **T40 SetCCMAttr, vendor comparison (no code change):** the OEM `tisp_ccm_params_refresh` (0x6ec74) copies only 4 bytes (`li a2,4`, 0x6ed48..0x6ed98) of the manual matrix into each of the six CT matrices, so on the vendor stack a manual CCM changes only the R->R coefficient; `tisp_s_ccm_attr` (0x718f8) also clears SatEn for the CCM and hands {ManualEn, SatEn, identity matrix} to `tisp_bcsh_set_attr` while neither CCM (TOP bit 9) nor BCSH (bit 15) is bypassed. The open driver applies all nine coefficients (beyond vendor) and keeps SatEn in the CCM.

## 2026-10-25 (4): T31 `isp_memopt` defaults to 1 (`claude/t31-memopt-default`; T31L fleet and T31X device-tested)

- **T31: `isp_memopt=1` is now the module default** (`0` = off, as before). Until now every T31 board needed `isp_memopt=1` on the module line to get the smaller ISP memory layout (MDNS allocation `0x2f8740`, fits `rmem=22M`); without it the module ran the larger OEM layout. Device evidence: the T31L fleet (.26 and others) has run `isp_memopt=1` since the stripe fix; additionally the T31X Wuuk (.21, sc4336p, 2560x1440 sensor, agg46) was reloaded from `isp_memopt=0` to `1` (rmmod sensor + ISP, insmod with `isp_clk=200000000 print_level=1 isp_memopt=1`, streamer restarted): 10 min, snapshot always 200, 0 oops, no VIC/ISP error in dmesg, row/column-mean discontinuities of the 1920x1080 snapshot identical to the `isp_memopt=0` frame of the same scene (rows max 18 vs 17, columns max 21 vs 20-21), day -> night once through `image.running_mode` without error. The module line `isp_memopt=1` in images stays valid; boards that want the old layout set `isp_memopt=0`.

## 2026-10-25 (3): T40 SetISPRunningMode no longer reports a false error (`claude/t40-lit1025`; Eufy T40 device-tested on a test ko)

- **T40: the running-mode ioctl returned the day/night refresh status (1) after a successful switch**, so libimp and the application logged `SetISPRunningMode(day) failed (rc=1)` on every day/night change although the mode was applied (isp-m0 and the kernel log showed the new mode). The ioctl now returns 0 unless the status is negative. Device (.184): running_mode 1 then 0 through the streamer, no `failed` line in the log, mode follows, 0 oops.

## 2026-10-25 (b): `t31_mxu2_cpm` parameter text reworded

- **T31: no longer calls CPM 0x10000034 an MXU2 gate.** The `MODULE_PARM_DESC` of `t31_mxu2_cpm` now says "CPM 0x10000034 read by the selftest, information only; it is a clock/part register". Text only, no code change (builds as before; host tests unchanged).

## 2026-10-25 (2): T40 AE runs with a sub-stream-only consumer (`claude/t40-dn1025`; Eufy T40 device-tested on a test ko)

- **T40: AE/AWB no longer freeze while only the sub stream (or nothing on the main stream) is consumed.** The frame 3A sampled channel-0 completions only. With an on-demand FrameSource a ch1-only client (20 s ch1 RTSP: 0 new samples) left the exposure at its last value, so after an IR-off day switch the streamer read the IR-lit exposure (554 after 8 s, the real 22117 only 14 s later, after a main-stream client came) and decided "day" in the dark; the IR flapping then disturbed the neighbouring cameras. Channel 1 now feeds the same AE/AWB/EV loop whenever channel 0 has been idle for `frame_3a_ch1_idle_ms` (default 300 ms, 0 = old behaviour, counter `frame_3a_ch1_samples`). Device (.184, dark, ch1 only, `daynight day`): EV 3375 -> 286830 (max) in ~3 s, the streamer switched back to night after ~16 s and the AE settled back in ~4 s; stock: no change at all. With a main-stream client the AE was already fast (4353 -> 286830 in ~3 s). Without any consumer the ISP stream is off on this camera (`framesource.keepalive=0`), so nothing can be measured then; that case stays the streamer's (keepalive / freshness check).
- Day -> night -> day saturation (seen on agg43d): on agg46 the BCSH registers the BCSH chain writes (0x1103c..0x11070) come back bit-identical to the boot day at the same EV (device dump, dark scene), and a host run of the literal BCSH chain over 3 day/night/day cycles shows no drift (`tests/t40/bcsh_dn_host`). The colour check in light is still open (light was unavailable).

## RC2 (v2026.10.25-rc2, 2026-10-25; open-tx-isp next 1bf4549d + docs, with OpenIMP next 7c30aa99 + docs)

Release note with the full per-SoC text: [OpenIMP docs/RELEASE_NOTES_RC2.md](https://github.com/opensensor/openimp/blob/aperto/docs/RELEASE_NOTES_RC2.md); rendered feature matrix: [feature-matrix.html](https://htmlpreview.github.io/?https://github.com/opensensor/openimp/blob/aperto/docs/feature-matrix.html). Use matching tags of both repositories.

- 636 open-tx-isp commits since RC1 (`v2026.10.11-rc1`). The exact code runs on all 19 test cameras as image agg46; the overnight soak of 2026-10-24/25 on the T40 and T41 cameras was clean (0 oopses, snapshots always 200).
- **T41:** defog root cause fixed and on by default, DRC/DPC/NR, CCM/gamma/CSC, live crop envelope 1920x1080, day/night apply from the AE statistics interrupt, module reload without oops or leak, size switches.
- **T40:** boot-time oops, reload/unload and dead-client release fixed; AE, AWB, DRC/Sinter/Temper ratios, CSC to BCSH and the night picture against the vendor driver (A/B); tuning-control oops fixes.
- **T31:** AWB colour-temperature detection on the MXU2 vector unit by default (`t31_awb_impl=2`, load-time self-test, bit-exact); front crop enlarges up to 2.0x (`fcrop_upscale_pct=100`); AWB interrupt stack use removed; ISP interrupt quiesce before `request_irq` (also T23, T21).
- **Changed defaults:** `t41_defog=1`; T41 and T40 bring-up/diagnostic parameters only with `TX_ISP_T41_BRINGUP_PARAMS=y` / `TX_ISP_T41_TMO_SELFTEST=y` / `TX_ISP_T40_BRINGUP_PARAMS=y` / `TX_ISP_T40_DEBUG_PROC=y`; `t31_awb_impl=2`; `fcrop_upscale_pct=100`; `t40_dmsc_follow=1`.
- **Known limitations:** T31 long-term stability (one unexplained reboot on 2026-10-17, one hang on the old agg43 image; multi-day run ends 2026-10-29); T40 manual CCM effect small, sharpness 4-5 % below the vendor; T21 IRQ quiesce for the VIC is QEMU-tested only.

## 2026-10-25: T21 IRQ quiesce also parks the VIC (QEMU-tested, not yet on a camera)

- **T21: reload after streaming no longer livelocks.** The first quiesce version (VIC mask 0xffffffff left in place, core mask restored) was not enough on QEMU: the VIC kept latching a frame-done status every frame after the previous load (the VIC command register +0x0 is never written on rmmod), the half-initialised handler never acked it, and the level IRQ still livelocked `request_irq` (soft lockup in `__do_softirq`, reload cycle 1..4). Now `isp-w02` is reset first (+0x0 = 2, the same value `tx_isp_vic_start` writes before 1), the status is acked, and the mask register is restored to its old value instead of being left at 0xffffffff (a permanently masked VIC delivers no frame IRQs). The core int enable (+0x80) stays 0 after the quiesce; the stream on/off path owns it (0x283f03ff / 0). QEMU T21 (PC420 image, jxf23): 10 reload cycles after the streaming application had streamed, no lockup, no "nobody cared", ISP frame IRQ counter keeps rising after the streaming application restarts and a snapshot is served. T23 (Cinnado D1, sc2336) with the unchanged 5879744d quiesce: 10 cycles, no "nobody cared", counter rising.

## 2026-10-24 (11): T40 night picture: DMSC follows the night bank and gain (`claude/t40-night-lift`; Eufy T40 device-tested on a test ko)

- **Night picture no longer lifts mid-tones twice as much as the vendor (T40):** with the default profile the demosaic block (DMSC, 0xa000..) ran on, but nobody re-interpolated it, so at night it kept low-gain day values. The literal OEM DMSC chain (active bank + total gain, the same as `tisp_dmsc_all_reg_refresh`) now runs at night. A host run against the sc830ai bin reproduces the vendor night block bit for bit (`tests/t40/dmsc_night_host`). On the day return the saved day DMSC block is written back, because the day bank desaturated the picture in this pipeline. Device (.184, dark + IR850, max gain, same scene): luma / p50 / temporal noise 31 / 25 / 2.08 (stock) -> 20-22 / 8 / 1.10-1.14 (test ko), vendor 24 / 11 / 1.25; the top end is unchanged (p99 104-114, vendor 111). Day before night: chart dE00 5.1, chroma 1.06 (vendor 5.2-5.3 / 0.99). `t40_dmsc_follow=0` restores the old behaviour.
- Known, not from this change: on stock agg43d a day -> night -> day cycle leaves the day picture desaturated (HSV S 15 against 44) until reboot. It was seen with this change and with `t40_dmsc_follow=0` too.

## 2026-10-24 (11): QEMU findings: T40 ISR slot index, ISP IRQ quiesce on reload (`claude/qemu-fixes`; QEMU-tested, not yet on a camera)

- **T40: the ISR frame-drain leg no longer reads a wild address.** `tx_isp_core_probe` fills each 216-byte frame slot with `+116 = idx % 3`, `+120 = idx / 3` (vendor `div` at 0x7a5d8); the lift dropped the division and stored the stale register (`&isp_core_tuning_init`, a module pointer) in both words. The ISR (`lw 0x17c(core + slot[+120]*96)`, vendor 0x7b550) then used the pointer as pipeline index and loaded from a wrapped address. QEMU delivers frame IRQs and bus-errors on the unmapped read (every run, ~57 s after the streaming application start); real silicon returns junk for it, the value only fed a diagnostic word, which is why the Eufy never faulted. The index is now correct and clamped to 0..2 at the read. (The probe loop's `uint32_t *` counters still step in 4-byte units; only slot 0 is initialised for the Eufy, unchanged and noted for a later cleanup.)
- **T31/T23/T21: pending ISP interrupt at reload.** A VIC/ISP status bit latched by the previous stream fired into the freshly registered isp-w02 handler (T31/T23: "irq 38: nobody cared / Disabling IRQ #38", ISP IRQs dead until reboot; T21 in QEMU: soft lockup in `__do_softirq`). Mask + ack of the interrupt registers now runs before `request_irq` (T31/T23 VIC 0x1e8..0x1f4, T21 VIC 0x104/0x108 and core 0x80/0x88) and, on T31/T23, before the IRQ is freed.

## 2026-10-24 (10): T40 Temper ratio works, CSC follows the vendor flow (`claude/t40-csc-temper`; Eufy T40 device-tested on a test ko)

- **`SetModule_Ratio` Temper (3D NR) strength works on T40** (before: stored only): hand translation of the vendor's `tisp_s_mdns_ratio` on the literal MDNS state. Ratio 0..128 scales the bin's 7 temporal-NR strength curves (11 gain nodes each) down linearly, 129..255 raises them towards caps of 230/250; the bin values stay untouched (the ratio is absolute), then the MDNS registers are reprogrammed at the current gain. Like the vendor it is applied again after the day/night MDNS reload. Device (.184, 3 runs): ratio 0/128/255 return 0 with readback, 28-35 of the MDNS registers (0xf000..0xf3ff) change per step, 0 oops; frame-to-frame noise 1.16/0.82/1.08 (ratio 0 clearly noisier, 255 not quieter than 128 in this lit scene). Host test `tests/t40/mdns_ratio_host`.
- **`SetISPCSCAttr` now does what the vendor's does:** `tisp_csc_set_attr` (CSC registers 0xd010..0xd030) plus `tisp_bcsh_csc_attr` (gamut selects the BCSH clip/offset/matrix set, BCSH refresh), and `tisp_set_csc_version` is hand-translated (the lifted body lost the register base shift). On the device the BCSH registers follow the gamut (clip 0..1023 full, 64..940/876 limited, Y offset 0x440), but the channel 0 picture does not change (luma sd 55.9 / 55.8 for BT601 full / limited) and the CSC registers 0xd010.. read back the BT709-full init values whatever is written (0xd000 reads 0). Open: whether the vendor shows an effect here needs a vendor register dump of 0xd000..0xd03c / 0x11000..0x11070.
- New state lives in section `.t40c2`; `.rodata`/`.data`/`.bss` sizes are unchanged against `next`.

## 2026-10-24 (9): T40 module reload and streamer restart without oops or leaks (`claude/t40-reload`; Eufy T40 device-tested on a test ko)

- **`rmmod tx_isp_t40` works on T40** (before: oops on the first unload, module stuck "Unloading", reboot needed; the Eufy history of unload hangs is closed): the lifted `private_wake_up_all` woke a NULL wait queue in the frame-channel cancel of the framesource remove; the framesource device is now removed before the core (its queue cancel sends events to the core pads).
- Unload frees what the probes allocated (core object and channel array, tuning object, VIN and framesource objects; the lift passed NULL to every one of these frees), removes `/proc/jz/isp/isp-m0` (a stale entry kept the unloaded module's fops and blocked the next load's mkdir) and drops the global pointers to the freed objects.
- The sensor i2c client is released on unload; a stale client of the same sensor (older build, stock module, crash) is adopted instead of failing with `-EBUSY`, so the next unload releases it.
- **Streamer restart leak closed (beyond the open driver's previous state, like the vendor):** each streamer stop/start leaked ~36 vmalloc areas / ~344 KB (64 MB Eufy: out of memory after ~60 restarts) because the per-session teardown (`tisp_deinit`, run by the vendor at every session end) is skipped. ADR and LCE are torn down before their per-session re-init; a vmalloc tracker frees an area when its call site allocates again in a later session (AE main init) and frees all lifted areas left at module exit (19 areas / ~230 KB per load before).
- Test (.184, agg43d, one boot): 10 rmmod/insmod cycles with streamer stop, 10 after `kill -9` of the streamer, 10 streamer stop/start: 30/30 snapshot 200, 0 oops, unloaded state vmallocinfo 29 areas / 1982464 B flat over 20 reloads, 92 areas flat over 10 restarts (stock: +36 per restart), ISP iomem/IRQs and the sensor client gone after every unload.

## 2026-10-24 (8): T40 CCM/CSC/GetGamma attributes, sensor-flip groundwork (`claude/t40-cells2`; Eufy T40 device-tested on a test ko)

- **`GetGammaAttr` works on T40** (answers from the programmed soft-gamma LUT: mode 0, 129 monotone nodes, 0 ... 4095).
- **`Set/GetCCMAttr` work on T40** on the literal CCM state (before: refused, the lifted body copied to address 0): manual flag, saturation flag and matrix are stored, `params_refresh` + `ev_ct_update` apply them; readback manual=1, m00=1.000 on the Eufy. Unlike the vendor (which copies 4 bytes of the manual matrix into each CT matrix), all 9 coefficients are copied. The visible colour change of a red-boost matrix was small (dV +0.7) - treat as functional, effect not yet characterised.
- **`Set/GetISPCSCAttr` no longer crash**: the T40 libimp block is 64 bytes (the OEM kernel stored 92: stack overrun, SIGSEGV in the caller); standards and the user matrix are programmed (registers 0xd010..0xd030), readback follows. No visible change on channel 0 between BT601 full/limited yet.
- Fixed a lifted `tisp_set_csc_version` that wrote its state 85 KB past an int module parameter at every `tisp_main_init`.
- Sensor mirror/flip (HVFLIP 4..8) on sc830ai: the vendor module has no flip event, so the vendor never flips this sensor. The core now writes the sensor's mirror/flip register itself from the HVFLIP ioctl, but the picture does not change yet (writes accepted, no visible effect) - still open.
- Note for T40 lift work: new static state must not be added to `.bss`/`.data` (the lift reaches the OEM layout through `&isp_memopt + N`); it lives in section `.t40c2`.

## 2026-10-24 (8): T40 SetScalerLv no longer oopses (`claude/t40-crop-pan`; Eufy T40 device proof)

- **`IMP_ISP_SetScalerLv` on T40 oopsed the kernel** (NULL + 8 in `tisp_set_scaler_level_control`): the lifted body lost the base of the attribute read and, for vinum 0, the register bases of three pointers (only the vinum 1 branch was right). Rewritten from the OEM disassembly (0x75308): weights for fitting-curve (mode 0, neutral 512/0) and level mode (mode 1, level 0..128 -> two weight pairs at 0x161c0..0x161cc + channel * 0x100 + vinum * 0x1000), enable check, load strobe; vinum or channel out of range -> -EINVAL. Device (Eufy .184, test ko): `SetScalerLv` ch0/ch1 mode 1 level 64 and ch0 mode 0 level 128 return 0 on the live stream, 0 oops, ch1 frame gap at most 77 ms. The picture effect was not measured (on T41 the curve levels showed no measurable change at a 4.5:1 scale).
- **AutoZoom on ch1 is live on T40:** ch1 640x360 window 1920x1080 at 960,540 applied during the stream, return 0, Get matches, region matches a crop of the full view (difference 9.9, threshold 15), frame gap 68 ms. The limits stay as the OEM hardware gives them (window not smaller than the channel output, 4K output only with the full window); the vendor library is a pure pass-through and the OEM kernel has no check, so the open driver is stricter (-1 instead of a stalled MSCA).
- Open: `Get/SetISPCSCAttr` (CID 0x8000096) still oopses on T40 (NULL + 0x14 in the CSC path, reproduced on the device); the guarded fix is in `claude/t40-wip-cells`, not duplicated here.

## 2026-10-24 (9): T40 night black level: GIB follows the total gain, pedestal traced to ADR (`claude/t40-quality4`; Eufy T40 night proof 17:40)

- **GIB black level vs gain:** the OEM tgain fan-out hands `tisp_gib_a_gian_update` the total gain (TotalGainDb includes the ISP digital gain, 7.0 log2 at night), so the bin's black-level nodes are read at node 7; with the analog gain only (quality2) the black level stayed too low at night and the 4x ISP stage amplified the remainder into a pedestal (Y p1 12..20 against the vendor's 7). Now the total gain is used; day unchanged (ISP dgain 1x).
- Night proof (.184, forced night, consumer on): p1 12 -> 11 only; the GIB black level registers are 0x104 at node 7 as at node 5 (the bin's tables are flat), so GIB is not the pedestal. Bypassing TOP bit 7 (ADR) by devmem drops p1 11 -> 5 (luma 78 -> 71); defog / LCE bypass leave p1 at 11. The night pedestal is the ADR chain lifting the shadows more than the vendor's (p1 7 with ADR on); open for the next round (ADR night bank refresh / ev-tgain scaling / ratio default).
- EV-unit probe (`t40_adr_ev_shift`, default 0, runtime): scaling the EV fed to the ADR chain by 1/64 or 1/1024 changes the night luma by +5 but not the black level (p1 10..11 at every shift; ADR bypass 5), so the ADR EV unit (plain integration x linear gain, as on T41 after af9b3519) is not the pedestal; whether the vendor's ADR lifts the night floor less in this scene needs a vendor A/B on the same scene.
- `SetModule_Ratio` defog entry runs the OEM `tisp_s_defog_ratio` on the private defog state. Temper (MDNS) stays cached only (the lifted `tisp_s_mdns_ratio` body uses isp_memopt-relative tables and is unsafe to call).

## 2026-10-24 (8): T40 AE EV fan-out, user AE caps, ratios (`claude/t40-quality3`; Eufy T40 device proof 17:00)

- **AE EV fan-out:** the OEM AE hands its EV (IT x gain, Q10) to defog, ADR, CCM and BCSH on every change and its total gain to ADR; ours fed only defog and the CCM/BCSH EV were module parameters frozen at 400, the ADR chain sat at its init EV/gain. `regtrace_t40_ev_fanout()` now runs per frame and on every sensor gain update (defog/ADR EV leaves, `ccm_ev`, BCSH EV, ADR tgain; read-only `t40_ev_now`, `t40_ev_follow=0` for A/B). This is the night -> day desaturation (CCM re-init at the stale EV) and part of the night black level (ADR without EV/gain follow lifts the shadows). `frame_3a_target` 118 -> 122 (vendor day luma 122).
- **User AE caps and AeTargetComp reach the open frame AE:** SetAeExprInfo max integration time / analog gain / ISP digital gain (mode 1) cap the AE, AeTargetComp scales the target (x comp/128) when enabled, GetAeScenceAttr reports the open AE's values (read-only `ae_user_max_*`, `ae_target_comp`). Before, the OEM setters only filled the lifted AE context the open AE never reads (T40_WIPCELLS_1024.md).
- **SetModule_Ratio:** DRC ratio runs the OEM `tisp_s_drc_ratio` translated into the literal ADR chain; the Sinter ratio reloads the literal SDNS tables from the scaled bank and replays the gain (it had no effect before). Temper/DPC/defog ratios stay cached only.
- Device proof (.184, stock agg43d against the branch, same scene with the colour chart, stream consumer on, 0 oops): day chart dE00 5.4 -> 4.7 (chroma 1.03 -> 1.05), day luma 117.4 -> 117.9, night luma 70 -> 79.5 (vendor 79), night black p1 5-8 (stock, AE not converged) / 12 (branch; was 20 on the old scene, vendor 7), saturation after the night -> day return 47 / 45 (no desaturation in either run with a consumer), `t40_ev_now` follows ExposureValue. Open: night p1, Temper ratio, HLC/BLC.

## 2026-10-24 (8): T31 front crop upscales like the vendor, AWB interrupt stack (`claude/t31-crop`; Cinnado D1 T31L .29)

- **T31 front crop now enlarges a window like the vendor:** the vendor accepts every window and lets the MSCA enlarge it (its T31 development guide documents enlarging an image); we refused any window smaller than a channel's scaler output (`fcrop_upscale_pct` default 0, -EINVAL). The default of `fcrop_upscale_pct` is now 100 (2.0x per axis, the clamp that was already there: 2.0x ran clean on the T31X, 2.5x hard-hung the SoC). Windows beyond 2.0x are still refused with -EINVAL and one log line (the vendor would stall the MSCA). `fcrop_upscale_pct=0` restores the old behaviour. Host test `tests/tx_isp_t31_fcrop_test` (default value, 1920x1080 + 640x360 under 960x540, 768x432 refused).
- **Device proof on .29 (T31L sc2336, 1920x1080 unscaled main):** 960x540 window (2.0x) set rc 0, picture zoomed (centre-crop SSIM 0.85 against the 1.0x picture, scene changed meanwhile), held 80 s, ISP 117 irq/s, snapshots 200 every 10 s, 0 oops; 600x338 (3.2x) and 768x432 (2.5x) refused -EINVAL with the log line, crop stays on the previous window; off restores 1920x1080; 1280x720 on and off clean.
- **AWB statistics interrupt without a 900-byte stack buffer:** `tisp_ae_mean_update` copied the 225 AE zones to the stack in hard-IRQ context (about 1.1 KiB frame chain on the 8 KiB task stack, MIPS 3.10 has no IRQ stack); it now sums them in place (`tisp_ae_get_y_zone_sum`, same mean). Hardening for the unexplained Wintergarten reboot of 2026-10-17 (analysis in the topic file `T31_LAST_CELLS_1024.md`); the cause is not proven.

## 2026-10-24 (8): T40 framechan freed when its client dies (`claude/t40-stability`; Eufy T40 device-tested on a test ko)

- **No more "no video until reboot" after a crashed client on T40:** the framechan release only dropped the file pointer; the OEM release streams off and frees the queue. A client killed while streaming (kill -9, SIGSEGV inside libimp) left the channel flagged as streaming and every later STREAMON (`0xc0045457`) returned -EBUSY. The release now streams off and frees the buffers when the closing file is the one that started the stream / allocated the buffers (another opener closing its fd leaves a live stream alone); a failed streamoff still clears the stale flag.
- Device proof (.184, test ko = wip-cells + next d1606e95 + this fix): stock agg43d after `kill -9` of the streaming application + restart: snapshot 503, 8x STREAMON -16 in 20 s; test ko: kill -9, SIGSEGV, kill -9 again: application back with snapshot 200 each time, 0x -16, 0 oops.

## 2026-10-24 (7): T40 lifted-body crashes in the tuning controls (`claude/t40-wip-cells`; Eufy T40 device-tested on a test ko)

- **Kernel oops / memory overwrite fixed or refused on T40** (each reproduced on the Eufy sc830ai with `t40_devtest`): `SetModuleControl` (LCE clear/top-state read NULL tool_para blocks; BadVA 8; the client then left the framechan degraded), `SetMask`/`GetMask` (NULL state base, NULL copy buffer), `SetWdrOutputMode` (lifted setter walks the missing WDR param array; now a stored flag per sensor, read back), `Get/SetISPCSCAttr` and `Get/SetCCMAttr` (92/40-byte copies into 4-byte slots, address 0; refused with -EOPNOTSUPP unless `t40_csc_unsafe=1`).
- `GetSensorFPS` answers the rate the set recorded (was the boot rate: 12 fps delivered, 20/1 read back).
- Device proof (test ko = next 091e1fa2 + these patches, 3 runs, 0 oops): LSC bypass through ModuleControl (corner/centre luma 0.971 -> 0.836), ISP flip modes 4/4, mask cover (Y 146 -> 255), WdrOutputMode 0/1/2 readback, SensorFPS 12/1.
- Still open on T40: AeTargetComp/HLC/BLC and AE caps are stored but never reach the open AE; Sinter/Temper/DRC ratios are stored without visible effect; CCM/CSC are refused; sensor flip modes do not change the picture.



## 2026-10-24 (8): T40 module size (`claude/t40-size2`; Eufy T40 device-tested)

- **T40 module size:** bring-up parameters (340 experiment switches and diagnostic counters that no tool, script, document, OpenIMP or thingino file uses; 288 parameters remain) and the legacy `/proc/tx_isp_t40_recovered` debug file (register dump, `exit N`, `dumplog`) are built in only on request: `TX_ISP_T40_BRINGUP_PARAMS=y`, `TX_ISP_T40_DEBUG_PROC=y` (Kbuild, default `n`). Stripped module 1557 -> 1445 KB, xz 431 -> 404 KB (.text 1023 -> 987, .rodata+strings 135 -> 103, .modinfo+__param 37 -> 16 KB). Variables keep their defaults, so behaviour is unchanged; passing a removed parameter to `insmod` only logs "unknown parameter ignored". Device (.184, test ko via the autostart swap, restored to stock agg43d afterwards): insmod OK, sc830ai probe, application up, snapshot and 2 MB of stream, 0 oops/warn. Host: `make -C tests check` 95 passed.

## 2026-10-24 (7): T40 defog / LCE / DPC chains, GIB gain follow (`claude/t40-quality2`; Eufy T40 device A/B)

- **T40 TOP word against the vendor:** the vendor runs `0x7FD9004D` on the sc830ai in daylight, our driver `0x7FF9089D`: four blocks the bin enables stayed bypassed in every profile: DPC (bit 4), ADR (7), defog (11), LCE (21). The recovered defog lift never hooked IRQ 10 / event 4 (its init calls the ISR with `(10, &isr)` in place of `system_irq_func_set`), the LCE lift is fragmentary. That is where the softer, flatter picture came from (black level up, contrast and night luma down).
- **Literal OEM chains** `t40_defog_on`, `t40_lce_on`, `t40_dpc_on` (`tools/gen_t40_defog_lit.py`, `gen_t40_lce_lit.py`, `gen_t40_dpc_lit.py`, same scheme as the ADR chain: private state, emulated MIPS stacks, runtime call dispatch; 0 unhandled opcodes / 0 unmapped). Init in the block init, TOP bit cleared only after a successful init, ISR/event hooks with counters (`dfglit_*`, `lcelit_*`, `dpclit_*`), day/night refresh and exit deinit like ADR. The sensor gain update feeds `tisp_lce_tgain_update`, `tisp_dpc_refresh` (16.16 log2 total gain) and `tisp_defog_ev_update` (IT x gain, Q10) like the OEM AE fan-out.
- **GIB black level follows the gain:** with the OEM GIB flow nobody called `tisp_gib_a_gian_update` after the init, so the black level stayed at the init gain interpolation (night Y p1 16 against the vendor's 7).
- Eufy .184 A/B (measure.sh open184e defog + LCE, open184f all four chains; 0 oops, ISR/process per frame, `*_bad_call` 0): day edge energy 4.69 / 5.02 (vendor 5.23, before 3.76), day contrast std 53.3 / 49.6 (vendor 50.8, before 47.0), day luma 115 / 120 (vendor 122, before 119), day black p1 17 / 15 (vendor 17, before 26); night luma 82 / 88 (vendor 79, before 66), night std 36.6 / 37.1 (35.6, before 23.9), night edge 3.0 / 2.8 (2.4, before 1.7); grey balance 7/4 % and 1.5/0.9 % (vendor 12/11 %). TOP with all four: `0x7FD9000D` (vendor `0x7FD9004D`). **Defaults:** `t40_adr_on`, `t40_defog_on`, `t40_lce_on`, `t40_dpc_on` = 1.
- **AE gain ceiling from the sensor attribute** (`ae_max_again_from_sensor`, default 1; read-only `t40_ae_max_again_index_eff`): the AE gain ladder is the GC4653 one (index 25 = 76x) while the sc830ai stops at 32x (attribute max_again 5.0 log2); indices 21..25 were clamped silently by the sensor but reported (AeAGain 77644 at night) and fed to the gain-indexed tables. The ceiling is now the highest ladder step within the attribute's max_again, so the ISP digital gain stage starts where the sensor stops, like the OEM. Device re-test (night) pending.
- **Framechan registration kept idempotent** (mirror of the T41 a0449408 fix): `tx_isp_frame_chan_misc_register` returns when the channel's registered flag is set. On T40 the nodes are registered once at probe and the OEM CREATE_FRAME_CHAN path is not on any ops table, so this is a guard only (code review, no device test).
- Open: night black level p1 20 (vendor 7; the gain ceiling is active on the device, night A/B pending), colour saturation after the night -> day return (S 15, vendor 51; reproducible with running_mode 1 -> 0 in daylight, not BCSH: a user saturation set moves S 15 -> 21 -> 15; also before this branch), no second-owner guard on T40 sensor register (T41 b8aa24a3).

## 2026-10-24 (7): T41 defog root cause fixed and defog on by default; T41 size switches (`claude/t41-defog-fix`, `claude/t41-size`; .32 and .34 device-tested)

- **T41 defog: diamond lattice fixed, default ON (`t41_defog=1`).** Root cause: the translated defog chain read its constant tables from `vae_rodata`, the rodata image of the vendor-AE translation (15760 B), but the chain was translated from a module whose `.rodata` is 14784 B; the defog tables sit 1056 bytes apart in the two images, so `defog_count_weight35abc` scaled garbage into the curve registers (0xc020..0xc08c) and the five weight tables (0xc098..0xc12c, which saturated into a 0x01/0x13/0x1F plateau instead of 0..0x0d). The saturated-weight zone drew a bright diamond (+11..+18 grey levels) at every block corner. Fix (`cfdd1420`): `driver/t41/tx_isp_t41_dfg_rodata.inc` (the 14784-byte image) and `tx_isp_t41_dfg_gen.inc` using it; `tools/t41_vae/gen_defog.sh` regenerates both. Result on a gc5603 (2880x1620, blocks 160x162, bright light): defog registers 0xc000..0xc28c identical to the vendor dump (0 differences, before 64), diamond metric -0.7 with defog on / -0.9 off (before +8..+18), mean luma 138.2 vs 138.8, contrast std +7.5 %, Laplacian +10 %, `defog_irqs == frames`, no oops. The default is switched on in `091e1fa2` (supersedes "Defog off by default" of 2026-10-24 below). The diagnostic hooks used for the search were reverted. This also removes the night softness against the vendor (the lifted blacks without defog); noise reduction was not the cause. Not tested: the strength sweep through `Module_Ratio` index 4 on a haze scene.
- **T41 module size (`2b4740a6`):** the new switch `TX_ISP_T41_TMO_SELFTEST` (builds the `t41_tmo_map_selftest` parameter) and the existing `TX_ISP_T41_BRINGUP_PARAMS` both default to `n` (the module-reload heap overwrite they were kept for has not reappeared in 70+ load/unload cycles). Stripped module 885 -> 836 KB (vendor 778 KB), xz 314 -> 297 KB (vendor 226 KB), RAM -34 KB. Device (T41, sc5336): 5 reload cycles, 0 oops/warn, AE identical to the previous module. Scripts that read the bring-up parameters (`tools/rawchart/t41_isr_stages.sh`) need a build with `TX_ISP_T41_BRINGUP_PARAMS=y`; the self-test needs `TX_ISP_T41_TMO_SELFTEST=y`. The remaining gap to the vendor module is function the vendor module does not have (native AWB/ADR/LCE/TMO/mdns, translated AE and defog).
- Also in `next` since the second pass: `t41_msca_live_max` default 1920x1080 (entry 5), T40 picture (entry 6), T31 AWB MXU2 default after a load-time selftest (entry 4).
- **Open (T41):** two reboots of one camera and one of another while streaming 1080p + 360p at 25 fps 8000 kbit/s on the open stack (cause not found, not related to stream buffer sharing; the investigation is running).

## 2026-10-24 (6): T40 picture against the vendor (`claude/t40-quality`; Eufy T40 device A/B)

- **T40 (Eufy sc830ai): vendor AWB per frame on by default** (`enable_core_ops_cb=1`): the OEM core ISR now reaches the AWB statistics interrupt and runs the translated vendor AWB each frame. A/B against the vendor stack on the same scene: R/G 1.85 -> 1.03, B/G 0.70 -> 1.00, grey-patch channel deviation 53 % -> 12 % (vendor 12 %), saturation 162 -> 53 (vendor 51). The lifted OEM AE that the same ISR would start is gated (`t40_oem_ae`, default 0; it oopsed in `tisp_ae_long_tune2`), and the frame-3A AE sampler ticks from the frame-done send (own work item) because the OEM ISR delivers frames in hard-IRQ context.
- **T40 AE:** integration ceiling from the sensor attribute (2241 lines, was 1919), target 118, two gain steps per sample far from target, gain bands against the limit cycle a 1.17x gain step caused inside the deadband. Light step 100 -> 255 settles in < 1 s (vendor 1.5 s, before 11 s). Day luma 127 (vendor 122, before 108), day temporal noise 1.06 (vendor 1.90).
- **T40 ISP digital gain, tuning follow, YSP:** the AE adds up to 4x ISP digital gain through the OEM GIB entry once the sensor gain is exhausted (night luma 24 -> 66, vendor 79; light step to 100 holds 124, vendor 105); the per-frame tuning follow (DNS/YSP/LSC/CCM/BCSH) now runs on the OEM ISR delivery path too and the BCSH saturation is re-applied after a night -> day return; YSP enabled by the bin gates (`t40_ysp_on=1`, edge energy 2.89 -> 3.76, vendor 5.23).
- Open on T40: sharpness (-28 % edge energy against the vendor), night luma -16 %. 0 oops in 8 device runs (~2 h stream in total).

## 2026-10-24 (5): T41 live crop envelope 1920x1080 (`claude/t41-crop`; .34 device ladder)

- `t41_msca_live_max` default 768x432 -> 1920x1080: live AutoZoom/front crop reprogramming on ch1 up to 1280x720 and ch0 1920x1080 (from the 2880x1620 sensor) ran on .34 without hang or latch error (`msca_ctl_failed` 0, max frame gap 70 ms, ch0 window SSIM 0.91 against the matching crop). Larger requests are still deferred to the next stream start.

## 2026-10-24 (4): T31 AWB colour-temperature detection: fast C and MXU2 (`claude/t31-mxu2-stats`; device-tested on T31X and T31L)

- **`t31_awb_impl`** (0 = reference C; 1 = fast C; 2 = MXU2, default: used after a load-time selftest passed, fast C otherwise; 0/1 turn MXU2 off) for `Tiziano_Awb_Ct_Detect`, the per-frame AWB detection (event 10, the largest part of the 1.76 ms ISP event work). All modes are bit-exact (host test `t31_awb_simd_host_test`: 6000 random scenes, outputs and every intermediate array, plus 1.9 M fixed-point cases).
- Fast C: the shift/subtract fixed-point divide of every zone/mesh step becomes one or two hardware divides, the Q multiply one 64-bit multiply, and the cluster loops compare precomputed zone coordinates. On a T31 (userspace run of the same code, synthetic scenes): 1668 -> 1072 us per detection.
- MXU2 (kernel mode only; read-only `t31_mxu2_cpm` shows CPM 0x10000034 = CPPSR, a clock-module part register that libimp uses to tell full parts from "-L" parts, for information only and not an MXU flag; the T21N sets CU2 but its MXU2 instructions are reserved, so no SIMD there, the vendor libimp never enables it on T21; selftest bit-exact on a T31X and a T31L, about 2.4x faster than C on the sweeps): the three 225-zone sweeps (cluster convergence, cluster count, zone-to-cluster assignment) run 4 zones per instruction. Kernel use without an MXU API on 3.10: only in a kernel thread with interrupts off (the OEM-style event dispatch), CU2 set around the section, the used VPRs cleared afterwards; any other context falls back to fast C. MXU2 is used only after `t31_awb_selftest` passed (writing `t31_awb_impl=2` starts it): it compares the kernels and whole detections against the reference on random scenes, times the three modes and survives a trapping instruction (DIE_RI guard) by failing instead of oopsing. Result in `t31_awb_selftest_state` and dmesg.

## 2026-10-24 (3): T41 day/night idle apply only without frame-completion IRQs (`claude/t41-ab-fixes3`; device re-test pending)

- The AE-statistics-interrupt apply of `t41_dn_apply_ae_irq` now runs only while no frame-completion interrupt (bit 0) arrived for 250 ms (idle camera); with a consumer the bit-0 path alone applies, exactly as before ab-fixes2. A/B on .32 showed the parameter itself changes neither the auto day return (about 35 s with 0 and 1) nor the day saturation after a night cycle; the reported misses came from the shared test light (.34 run in the same room) and from the timps reference timing.
- The night/day bank work waits up to 3 s for a first AE exposure before computing CCM/BCSH/TMO (an idle boot with a persisted night mode applied the bank with ev=0).

## 2026-10-24 (2): T41 day/night switch without a stream consumer (`claude/t41-ab-fixes2`; device re-test pending)

- **Day/night switch applied from the per-frame AE statistics interrupt:** the transition state machine (CSC fill, night/day parameter bank, TOP bypass) was stepped only from the frame-completion interrupt (status0 bit 0), which arrives while a consumer (stream, snapshot) is attached. On an idle camera a running-mode request flipped the readback at once but the bank switch waited 30..100 s for the next viewer (.32: dn-bank applies only at the snapshots; forced day left AWB at 5000 K and a red cast, the application logged "ISP did not follow the switch"). The AE statistics interrupt (bit 4) comes every frame without a consumer; `t41_dn_apply_ae_irq=0` restores the old trigger.

## 2026-10-24: T41 A/B fixes against the vendor stack (`claude/t41-ab-fixes`; .32 gc5603 A/B, device re-test pending)

- **Tone consumers got the EV 1024x too large:** gamma, CCM, BCSH, ADR and the TMO curves compare the exposure against calibration knots in plain integration x linear gain units (gc5603 bin: gamma 50..200000, CCM/BCSH 32..102400, ADR 160..4800, TMO 20..300000; stock gets the AE event shifted by 10 and shifts it back). The tone worker passed the AE-precision value (x1024), which put them in their lowest-light bracket at any brightness: gamma strength 128 instead of 252 (day Y 103 instead of about 135: 24 % darker than the vendor stack), CCM/BCSH saturation from the dim-light bracket (28 % lower). The worker now hands them the plain EV; AWB keeps its own unit. The AE precision (params +0x6c0) is read from the calibration instead of assumed.
- **TMO local map on MXU3 by default** (`t41_tmo_map_impl=2`, bit-exact with the C reference, falls back to C without MXU3): the per-frame tone worker was the main system-CPU difference to the vendor stack.
- **ISP interrupt sources trimmed to what is consumed** (`t41_isr_enable`: all but AF bit 6 in 0x40020, LSC/BCSH frame-down bits 4+20 in 0x400a0; `t41_isr_dispatch != 0` restores everything): isp-m0 215 -> 125 IRQ/s on .32 (vendor 179), frame rate and tone/AWB/ADR work unchanged.
- **Defog off by default (`t41_defog=0`; superseded by 2026-10-24 (7): root cause fixed, default on again):** the translated chain leaves a bright diamond at every block corner of the gc5603 day picture (18 x 10 lattice, scales with the defog strength; none with defog bypassed, none in the vendor stack). Init registers are identical on host and device and the per-block LUT is smooth, cause open.

## 2026-10-19: T40 tuning crash fixes (`claude/t40-sensorreg` up to 6b5dfac7; in `next`)

- **Sensor register ioctl / tuning s_attr:** the 8 tuning attribute setters copied user data into a 4-byte stack slot (stack smash, hard hang on SetAeExprInfo and similar). They now copy into a kernel buffer of the right size like the OEM, and the sensor register ioctl reaches the sensor.
- **Tuning attribute cache:** allocated on isp-m0 open like the OEM `tisp_enable_tuning` (NULL oops in `tisp_tattr` before).
- **Getters/setters rebuilt like the OEM:** AeExprInfo, the g_ctrl getters (copy_to_user from a kernel buffer), AE/AWB/AF weights, ModuleRatio/sdns ratio; the streamer-reachable oopses in SetAfWeight (`tisp_af_params_refresh`) and SetModule_Ratio are gone.
- Device-tested on the T40 (Eufy, gc4653): register rounds 50/50, Get-Set-Get identical for all attributes except gamma (needs `t40_module_ctx_inits=1`), AE hold 60-75 s, 0 oops. Open: the auto-mode exposure readback unit, the manual analog gain, and the follow-up commit d1c0d0f0 (OEM AE for GetAeExprInfo, ratio setters) stay on the branch.

Commits by Paul Philippov from Lu-Fi/open-tx-isp#2 (author kept, `cherry-pick -x`), adapted where noted.

Commits by Paul Philippov from Lu-Fi/open-tx-isp#2, author kept; the AK Sharma commits the PR carries again were already in `next`.
