## 2026-10-19: Takeover from AK Sharma's mainline T31 port (`claude/akoo7-port`)

Commits by AK Sharma (fork AKoo7/open-tx-isp, `t31-6.12-port-v2`), author kept.

- **T31 IRQ leak and double free after a failed core probe fixed (device-tested, T31L .29 sc2336, kernel 3.10.14):** `tx_isp_core_probe` freed the global `ourISPdev` on failure (then freed again by `tx_isp_exit`, IRQ never released); `tx_isp_subdev_init` returned straight out of the clock/pad failure paths and freed the IRQ with the wrong `dev_id`. Test: the shipped load order already fails the core probe benignly (`/dev/framechan0: -17`, pre-existing), i.e. the error path runs on every load; load, `rmmod`, reload, application streaming, 0 oops/WARN, no ISP IRQ left after `rmmod`.
- **GC4653 sensor for T31 (`sensor-src/t31/gc4653.c`): builds on 3.10.14, untested on hardware** (no T31 camera with GC4653). The mainline mbus aliases in `sensor-common.h` are now behind `LINUX_VERSION_CODE >= 5.0` (they broke the 3.10 sensor build).
- **Mainline 7.1/7.2 kernel-API adaptation merged with the k7-compat patch into one path** (`READ_ONCE/WRITE_ONCE`, `swait_active`, `.remove` void, `strlcpy`/`strncpy` shims, vendor clock-name aliases `cgu_isp->isp`, `csi->mipi_csi`, `cgu_cim->cim`, vendor-sized stand-in for the embedded `miscdevice` and no `spinlock_t` inside the OEM ABI window, no `__module_address`/`set_fs`/`__ioremap` in guard/trace). All behind `LINUX_VERSION_CODE`; the mainline path was **not build-tested** here (no 5.15+/7.x tree). 3.10.14 / 4.4.94 modules unchanged: `tx-isp-t31.ko` and `tx-isp-t23.ko` byte-identical, `tx-isp-t41.ko` identical apart from the git-describe string, `tx_isp_trace.ko` identical code (only srcversion).
- Not taken: `5ebcfb14` (flat first-sensor symlinks; our vendor `/proc/jz/sensor` layout already provides the flat files) and the T41 AWB scene sampling `50f529f9`.

## 2026-10-19: T41 defaults, T40 AWB statistics oops, T40 AE maximum integration (`claude/t41-defaults-vae-clm-tone`, `claude/t40-az-nohang`, `claude/t40-ae-maxint`; all in `next`)

- **T41 defaults changed (device-tested, T41 .32 gc5603):** `t41_vendor_ae=1` (translated vendor AE per frame), `t41_clm=1` (OEM CLM LUT by colour temperature) and `t41_tone_trigger=1` (per-frame tone worker from the AE statistics interrupt) are now on by default; `=0` restores the previous safe-AE / no-LUT / TMO-interrupt-only behaviour. Merged only after an overnight soak with exactly these settings: 16.6 h (2026-10-18 15:02 to 10-19 07:39) without reboot, 0 BUG/oops, the streamer ran without restart, day/night switched normally.
- **T40 oops in `GetAwbStatistics` fixed (device-tested, T40 .184):** every run of the user-space day/night thread hit an oops about 1 s after start: control `0x8000011` called the recovered `tisp_g_awb_statis_attr()` without arguments and `tisp_awb_get_zone` copied into an uninitialised pointer (BadVA 0). The oopsed thread died inside the tuning ioctl while holding the library's tuning lock, so every later tuning call (for example `SetAutoZoom`) blocked forever and the 3A thread was dead. `tisp_awb_get_zone` now follows the OEM (main/sec AWB tables, entries 125..127, 900 bytes each; plane stays zero before the first AWB frame), `tisp_g_awb_statis_attr(vinum, buf)` has the OEM signature, and the control fills a `kzalloc`ed 0xa8c-byte block and `copy_to_user`s it. Device proof: idle and live (ch0 1080p + ch1 360p) `SetAutoZoom` on/off answer in 10-30 ms with the region matching a centre crop, a 10 min run with two clients gave 9101/9100 frames, no PTS jump > 0.2 s, 0 oops, day/night thread alive. Earlier T40 results with a driver older than this fix are suspect (dead tuning after ~1 s).
- **T40 AE maximum integration like the OEM (device-tested, T40 .184):** (1) `tisp_ae_long/short_ctrls_update` compared against an unrelated stack local instead of the sensor limits; rewritten from the OEM disassembly (`tisp_ae_long_ctrls_update`, clamp of max/min integration time and gains against the sensor attributes). (2) `tisp_ae_main_ctrls`/`tisp_ae_sec_ctrls` were 4-byte objects but the OEM objects are 0xa4 bytes, so AE writes overflowed into neighbouring statics; storage fixed. (3) The sensor-trigger flag (`ae_cache[ch]+1292`) is now set and tested on the right objects, so a changed sensor FPS reaches the AE. (4) `SetSensorFPS` before the first delivered frame is kept pending and sent with the first exposure update (the OEM applies it from the frame-done work on a streaming sensor); a stale sensor attribute (integration limit 4657 at a 2250-line frame) from a set-FPS before stream-on was the cause of 11.6 fps. New read-only parameter `t40_ae_limits`. Result: sensor attribute and AE maximum 2241, 12/12 samples 19.9-20.4 fps, exposure <= frame length, 0 oops. Caveats: the order stream-on vs `SetSensorFPS` is racy, the pending path was reviewed but not hit in the final run; low light (gain instead of exposure at the cap) not yet shown. Not changed: `ae_cache` stride of the second sensor (0x514 vs OEM 0x518), `fs_work` dispatch, sensor-control stubs with fixed GC4653 values (separate items).

## 2026-10-19: Kernel 5.15 / 7.x compat for T31 (`claude/k7-compat`; in `next`)

- Taken over from thingino `feature/kernel-7` patch 0004 by themactep: `TX_ISP_VENDOR_TREE=1` builds the T31 driver and sensors against the Ingenic vendor 5.15.170 tree (no `set_fs`, own `timeval`/`PDE_DATA`, legacy mbus names, `gnu99`, no CGU binding header); mainline 7.x uses the existing >= 5.0 shims plus `ACCESS_ONCE`. Build-checked only (no 5.15/7.x tree locally, not device-tested). 3.10.14 and 4.4.94 are unchanged: T31/T41 code identical to before, T23 `.ko` byte-identical (T31 differs only in `__LINE__` constants).

## 2026-10-18: T40 restart, stop path and GIB (`claude/t40-gib-stop`, `claude/t40-restart-hang`; in `next`)

- **Second stream start with ch0+ch1 no longer hangs the SoC:** the last close of `/dev/tx-isp` now runs the OEM release path (idle handshake on 0x200/0x204 with a bounded wait, core reset pulse on 0x24) and the block init runs again per session; before, MSCA output 1 kept its state and the ISP bus froze ~60 ms after the next ch1 stream-on. Device-tested: 10/10 start/stop cycles with 181/181 frames on both channels, 10 min with two stream clients, 5 client reconnects, no oops. `t40_release_reset` (default 1) switches it off; difference to the OEM: clocks stay on.
- **Stop path like the OEM:** one `work_en` write after the 0x16010 latch, then 0 (before, 0x2 stayed and ch1 stayed on). An out-of-bounds byte write ~1.1 MB behind the MSCA state for ch1 (`a0*616 + &mscaler_s0`) and a stack overwrite in IspDg are fixed.
- **GIB block rebuilt from the OEM** (`tisp_gib_init` was a stub, `tisp_gib_ae_write_dgain` returned -1 every frame): with `t40_gib_real` auto it runs only together with `t40_gates_oem_offset=1` (device: init 0, dgain applied 598/598); with the default gates it stays off, because the black level would be subtracted twice. With offset 1 the black is lifted and the shadows turn blue, so the default picture stays unchanged (open).
- Diagnostics: `t40_fr_big` flight recorder at the end of rmem (survives a watchdog reset), default off.

## 2026-10-18: T40 substream, NV12 chroma and AutoZoom (`claude/t40-ch1`, `claude/t40-autozoom`; both in `next`)

- **ch1 delivers frames (`t40_isr_ch1_direct`, default 1):** the ISR pops MSCA output 1 on status0 bit 1 like the OEM ISR and the ch2 leg; before, ch1 stalled after its first buffer. Device (T40XP sc830ai): 1080p + 360p/720p 226/226 frames in 15 s, ch0 back to 15 fps; streamer 10 min, ch0/ch1 ~20 fps each, no oops.
- **NV12 UV offset (`t40_nv12_height_align=0` = auto):** UV at width*ALIGN(height,16) as the vendor driver when the queued buffer holds it, else width*height (older libimp). Removes the green strip at the bottom and the chroma shift on 360p.
- `tisp_gib_ae_write_dgain` no longer floods the log every frame; `t40_msca_late_work_en` (default 1) programs and latches the output geometry before work_en, in OEM order.
- **AutoZoom (CID 0x08000077) real on T40:** `tisp_s/g_autozoom_control` re-done after the OEM routine (per output channel a window, programmed into the MSCA source-window words at once, live while streaming; the recovered code had lost a relocation and oopsed). A window is accepted only when it lies inside the sensor, is at least 2x2 and not smaller than the channel output (the MSCA cannot upscale); otherwise `-EINVAL`, nothing stored. Second sensor (vinum 1) refused. Also fixed: after a stream restart the window position was lost (the OEM-order repeat wrote 0x160a0 = 0). Counters `t40_az_{live,deferred,stored,failed}`, optional deferral of large outputs via `t40_az_live_max_w/h`. Device-tested (T40XP sc830ai): outputs 640x360 to 2560x1440 live within one frame (gap 67-85 ms at 15 fps), regions match, invalid and too-small windows rejected, no oops. Output 4K only with the full window; zoom on ch1/ch2 not device-proven.
- Housekeeping: `fable-aeexp_test.c` (user-space GetAeExpList/SetAeExpList test) moved from the repository root to `tools/`.

## 2026-10-15: T40 integration branch `claude/t40-integration-1015` (not in `next`)

Open T40 driver on the T40XP board with the sc830ai sensor, built on `claude/t40-sc830ai` (06508ce5). Device run on that board: test application 24 results / 0 failures with the defaults and with `t40_gates_oem_offset=1 t40_gates_day_blc=1`, 10 minutes of streaming without an oops, memory flat. Every new behaviour below is opt-in (module parameter, default off) unless it says otherwise.

- **Lift defect classes fixed (default on, device-tested on the board):** OEM stack frames of further functions with width-aware slots (STACK_FRAMES2/3), class-1 relocations with the branch-order-risk sites left out (RELOC_CLASS1_HARD), pointer-typed operands in initialised declarations (PTR_DECL), the ISR loop stride (ISR_LOOP_STRIDE). The per-frame writer stack (OEM `tisp_hardware_reg_refresh`, gamma/CLM/LSC writer contexts, W-LUT producers) is included but stays behind its own opt-in.
- **Colour cast solved (`t40_gates_oem_offset=1 t40_gates_day_blc=1`):** the gate pass read the bin's bypass flags from the WDR set (+0x20) for a linear sensor; `t40_gates_oem_offset=1` reads +0x00 like the OEM (`t40_gates_reg6c=1` also takes TOP 0x6c from the active bank). With that mask BLC is bypassed and the GIB, which sits behind the white-balance stage, removes the black level after the gains have multiplied it: green/yellow cast with violet shadows (colour-saturation index 20). `t40_gates_day_blc=1` keeps BLC active in day mode and sets the GIB black level to `t40_gates_day_gib_blc` (default 0); night keeps the bin mask. Result on the board: index 7.9..9.7, no crushed blacks (the old pipeline: 8..9). Checked against the vendor module: BLC, GIB and SDNS registers equal the vendor values for the same bin; the vendor runs with TOP bit 2 set, which with this driver's white balance did not converge (reverted).
- **Vendor blocks as real functions, opt-in:** CDNS chroma denoise (`enable_cdns`, literal translation of the vendor code, TOP bit 19 cleared only after a successful init); YSP sharpening (`t40_ysp_on`; an init guard now keeps every refresh away from NULL tables when the init failed, which was the earlier "YSP panic" on boots without a tuning bin); ADR highlight compression (`t40_adr_on`, the 38 vendor functions of init, interrupt, event-2 process, register writers, day/night refresh and deinit translated literally with private state, own emulated stacks and runtime call dispatch; TOP bit 7 cleared only after a successful init; the forced ADR interrupt path is blocked while it is active). On the board all three initialise with return 0 and run without an oops; the per-frame ADR part waits for the core interrupt dispatch.
- **Diagnostics (default off):** `t40_gates_keep_blc`, `t40_gib_blc_delta`, `t40_awb_stats_ped`.
- **`tisp_day_or_night_s_ctrl` address fix:** the TOP register was addressed at 0x100 instead of 0x40 (pointer-typed index). The live day/night path does not use this function, so the fix is cold.
- **Tools and tests:** `tools/mips2c_literal.py` gained mult/madd/msub, full section addresses and a runtime-dispatch mode; `tools/gen_t40_adr_lit.py`, `tools/gen_t40_host_lit.py`; `tests/t40/adr_host_smoke` runs the literal ADR chain on the host against a tuning bin.

## 2026-10-14: aggregate agg-39 (draft, not released)

`claude/agg39` = `next` (agg-38, 17da5c8c) + `claude/t41-ch1`, `claude/t41-tone-worker`, `claude/t41-awb`, `claude/t41-wait-frame-done`, `claude/t41-audit` (via `claude/agg39-t41`) + `claude/t40-sc830ai` + `claude/log-quiet` + `claude/t21-i2c-cache`. The OpenIMP side is `claude/agg39` in openimp (`claude/t41-wfd-timeout`, `claude/t40-poll-timeout`, `claude/t21-os-revert`). Host tests (`make -C tests check`, 91/0) and module builds with the load check (`check_ko_symbols.sh`) for T10/T20/T21/T23/T30/T31/T40/T41; T10, T20, T23, T30, T31 are unchanged against agg-38. No soak of the aggregate yet. cam-F is the T41 gc5603 camera, the T40 camera is an sc830ai (3840x2160) board.

T41:

- **MSCA output geometry takes effect (`claude/t41-ch1`):** `t41_msca_cfg_update=2` and `t41_msca_stop_disable=1` are the new defaults, the CSC update request of `tisp_csccr_*_mode` is restored, and ch1 formats above `t41_msca_ch1_max_w` x `_h` (default 1280x720) are refused instead of hanging the SoC. cam-F: ch1 640x360..1280x720 correct (SSIM 0.993-0.996 against ch0), 30 min with ch1 960x540 plus 10 streamer restarts and 10 min with 1280x720 plus 5 restarts, no hang, no oops. `t41_msca_cfg_update=0 t41_msca_stop_disable=0` restores agg-38.
- **Per-frame tone worker, opt-in (`claude/t41-tone-worker`):** the worker (CCM/BCSH by EV, gamma, LSC, ADR, LCE, YSP, CDNS, MDNS, DPC, TMO) was only scheduled from a TMO interrupt that never fires on T41. `t41_tone_trigger=1` also runs it from the AE statistics interrupt; cam-F: every frame without errors, 30 min clean. Default stays 0 (the picture changes clearly and has not been compared with the vendor stack yet). ModuleControl refuses to take DEFOG out of bypass (-EOPNOTSUPP; the defog algorithm is not recovered).
- **AWB / EV (`claude/t41-awb`):** with the tone worker on, the EV is distributed in the stock units (it was 1024x too small, so every EV-indexed table used its bright-light entry and the AWB got a zero Y weight). cam-F, daylight: the detector runs, CT follows the light (2412 -> 3509 K under a warm lamp), TMO takes every frame; at night under IR the 5000 K gray fallback is the expected result. Read-only diagnostics `awb_diag`, `awb_diag_cal`, `awb_diag_raw`.
- **WaitFrameDone reports a timeout (`claude/t41-wait-frame-done`, deviation from the vendor driver):** a wait without a frame returns -ETIMEDOUT (a signal -ERESTARTSYS) instead of 0; the 24-byte block with the counter is filled in every case. The constant frame counter noted for agg-38 was not a counter bug: frames are counted while frame source channel 0 runs, and a streamer that stops its main channel without a client makes every wait time out (vendor behaviour too). cam-F: with channel 0 running 20/20 waits return 0 at 40 ms (25 fps), without it -ETIMEDOUT after the timeout. OpenIMP (`claude/t41-wfd-timeout`) returns -1 with errno ETIMEDOUT, runs the wait outside its library lock and logs no syslog line for timeouts; cam-F: a parallel `GetBrightness` takes < 0.1 ms during a wait (was up to 1780 ms of spinning).
- **Audit for the T40 recovery bug classes (`claude/t41-audit`):** `system_irq_func_set` is bounds-checked (`irq_func_cb_rejects`; vinum 0 stays within the stock 96 slots, a second sensor would have overflowed as in the vendor driver); 116 indirect calls that the recovery had resolved to `private_math_exp2` go through a counting wrapper with the old behaviour (`unresolved_jalr_count`, first callers logged); two kernel threads read `current` from address 0 (fixed, the threads are not started); 563 statements in reachable code added byte offsets to pointer-typed register variables and scaled them by 4 (cast to `uintptr_t`, offsets as in the stock code; mostly paths the open driver does not run: WDR, the stock AE behind the safe AE controller, suspend, raw dump). cam-F: 30 min with periodic stream pulls (25 fps, snapshots 200), 0 oops, both counters 0. The 48 sites of the follow-up (567763b4) are host-checked only.

T31/T41 log: missing optional T31 tuning files are an info line, and the bounded T41 IRQ/MSCA/running-mode/safe-AE traces only appear with `t41_runtime_trace=1` (`claude/log-quiet`, no functional change; host build and tests).

T21 (device-tested on the T21 jxf23 camera, night, runtime load, 60 s windows with one client):

- **Sensor exposure/gain written only on change, as stock (`claude/t21-i2c-cache`):** the vendor `sensor_set_integration_time` / `sensor_set_analog_gain` skip an unchanged value; the recovery queued both every frame. i2c IRQs 50-51/s -> 0-2/s, snapshot Y unchanged (96.7 -> 96.8-97.0), streamer restarts keep the picture: the cache is re-armed when the sensor reloads its init table (`t21_sensor_rearm_count`/`_writes`), `t21_sensor_write_always=1` restores the per-frame writes. AE after a light step is checked in the overnight soak (dusk/dawn).
- **OpenIMP Helix per-frame path back to -Os (openimp `claude/t21-os-revert`):** -O2 for these objects cost 18 KB of `.text` on T21/T23/T30 and bought no measurable CPU (streamer 9.3-10.1 % with -Os against 9.5-10.1 % with -O2).

T40 (`claude/t40-sc830ai`), device-tested on the sc830ai board at 3840x2160 with the T40 devtest (24 or 35 results, 0 failures unless noted) and no oops:

- **Bayer phase from the sensor format:** the CFA phase comes from the sensor mbus format (sc830ai BGGR) instead of GC4653 placeholders; full-frame U/V 129.9/128.0 without any override.
- **Fast AWB seed:** `frame_awb_bggr_fast` seeds the white balance from the sensor's BGGR calibration on the first statistics sample with a bounded trim (U/V 128.1/130.7); guarded to the calibrated 3840x2160 mode.
- **4K 3A window:** `frame_3a_slide_window` lets the 3A sampler catch every frame at native 4K (7/7 samples missed before, 0 after; frame Y 76.8 -> 153.3, neutral midtones).
- **Gamma from the tuning bin:** `t40_gamma_from_bin` uses the sensor bin's gamma instead of the GC4653 curve (shadow samples 181k -> 82k, frame Y 133.8 -> 158.1).
- **GetISPRunningMode** returns the mode (default 0, night 1, day 0; was left untouched); the AWB gain trace no longer dereferences a scratch pointer (user-space SIGSEGV with `enable_isp_block_init_awb=1`).
- **Defaults = best known-good line:** plain insmod with only `isp_clk` and the tuning bin path gives the tuned picture (profile 1, YSP bypass, DMSC static + overlay, 3A every third frame, sliding 3A window, BGGR seed, gamma from bin, frame size from the sensor): frame Y 184.5, neighbour difference 3.15. All parameters stay overridable.
- **Recovery bug fixes:** `irq_func_cb` grew from 16 to 80 entries (the secondary-bank slots 46/47/0x32/0x33 wrote into .bss) and `system_irq_func_set` is bounds-checked; 118 indirect calls resolved to `private_i2c_set_clientdata` (which wrote its second argument to the address in its first) go to a counting no-op (`unresolved_jalr_count` 0 in all runs); the ISR status loop and the core-ops dispatch call their real callbacks again.
- **Pointer scaling in the recovered code (06508ce5):** register variables declared as pointers had byte offsets added with element scaling (x4): statistic counters stepped by 4, a counter write landed 288 instead of 72 bytes past its base, ioctl command constants compared wrong, a loop over 16 subdevices ran 4 times, MSCA shadow-DMA register addresses and MDNS register values were off. The operand is cast in about 2600 sites of the reachable functions; 3 element steps were kept after a check against the stock code. Device run 2026-10-15: devtest 35 results, 0 failures, and 10 min with the streamer, no oops.
- **OEM stack frame in the frame-channel and core ioctls (06508ce5):** with the ioctl command constants correct, the stock QBUF branch copied 68 bytes onto a single 4-byte local (oops with epc = ra = 0); `frame_channel_unlocked_ioctl` and `tx_isp_unlocked_ioctl` now back their OEM stack slots with a real buffer, so struct copies land as in the stock frame. Same device run (devtest 35 results, streamer 10 min, 2026-10-15). The other functions of this class are listed in `driver/t40/audit/stack_frames.md` (branch `claude/t40-stack-frames-list`).
- **Opt-in per-frame hardware refresh:** `isr_oem_hw_refresh=1` calls `tisp_hardware_reg_refresh` from the frame-done path as the OEM ISR does (43 calls in about 4 s, ret 0); its WLUT helpers no longer read an unrelocated literal (Oops within 60 s before the fix). Default 0.

## 2026-10-13: branch `claude/t41-ch1` (not merged)

Module build against the agg-38 T41 kernel only; not run on a camera (device tests were not possible in this session).

- **T41 CSC update request restored:** stock `tisp_csccr_default_mode()`, `_range_stretch_mode()` and `_range_compress_mode()` end with `0xf0010 = 1`; the lifted bodies lost that tail call, so the CSC words (0xf08e0..0xf08ec, MSCA staged registers) never took effect and the CSC block stayed off (0xf08ec reads 0 on agg-38). The request is issued with `t41_msca_cfg_update >= 2` only (it latches every staged MSCA word); with the default 0 the words stay staged and are counted in `csc_latch_skipped`. Counters: `csc_latches`, `csc_latch_skipped`.
- **T41 ch1 size gate:** with `t41_msca_cfg_update >= 2`, SET_FMT on frame channel 1 larger than `t41_msca_ch1_max_w` x `t41_msca_ch1_max_h` (default 768x432, 0 = no limit) is refused with `-EINVAL` and a warning line, instead of hanging the SoC at the first start (960x540 and 1280x720 hung, see README "Output restart hang"). The scaler output size is checked too when the scaler is on. Counter `msca_fmt_refused`; host test in `tests/tx_isp_t41_msca_ctl_test.c`. No effect with `cfg_update=0`.
- **T41 MSCA defaults changed:** `t41_msca_cfg_update=2` and `t41_msca_stop_disable=1` are now the defaults (the MSCA output geometry, ratios and CSC really take effect; ch1 follows ch0 in colour and exposure, no band), and the ch1 gate defaults to 1280x720. Tested on T41 .32 with the module loaded at runtime: ch1 640x360..1280x720 correct (SSIM 0.993-0.996 against ch0); the streamer with ch1 960x540 for 30 min plus 10 restarts and with 1280x720 for 10 min plus 5 restarts, no hang, no oops. The client-driven idle-stop/restart (RTSP) was not tested. `t41_msca_cfg_update=0 t41_msca_stop_disable=0` restores the old behaviour.

## 2026-10-13: branch `claude/t41-awb` (on `claude/t41-tone-worker`, not merged)

- **Per-frame EV in stock units (host analysis, not yet run on a camera):** stock `tisp_ae_long_ev_alloc` pushes integration x total gain in the AE fixed-point precision (AE params +0x6c0, the same product the open AE target uses), and every stock `*_ev_update` (AWB, CCM, BCSH, gamma, ADR, TMO) only removes its own `<<10`. The tone worker shifted the precision away as well, so all of them saw a 1024x smaller EV: bright-light tables everywhere and, for the AWB, a zero Y weight for the only populated luminance bin. The worker now distributes the stock value (`tone_ev` reports it). Only active with `t41_tone_trigger=1`. The AWB bin thresholds (`set_regional_threshold` / `set_lum_th_freq`) were checked against the OEM code and match.
- **Device runs on T41 .32 (runtime load, cfg_update=2):** night mode with IR (basement, all day): EV now 407552-537600, AWB Y weight 5, zones counted, but the zone ratios (R/G 316, B/G 340 in Q8) lie off the calibrated white locus, so the surface weight is 0 and the gray fallback (5000 K, unity) is the expected result under IR; TMO returns -EAGAIN there (`tmo_again[0]`, empty statistics). 30 min under the streamer clean. Day mode (lamp on, IR cut in): the detector runs (flags 0x100), CT follows the light (2412 -> 3509 K, warm lamp), gains 732/348, TMO takes every frame (`tmo_again` 0). ch0 numbers with `t41_tone_trigger=1`: Y 95.6, R/G 1.134, B/G 0.876, temporal noise 1.17, detail 4.14; with 0: Y 103.7, R/G 1.127, B/G 0.792, noise 1.07, detail 3.90. The AWB also works with 0 in day light (the populated luminance bins then have calibrated weights); the earlier "always 5000 K" was mostly the IR night mode. Bayer phase: taken from the sensor format (gc5603 2880x1620@25 = SGRBG10 -> 0x88 phase 2), matches.

- **Why the T41 native AWB stays at 5000 K / constant gains** (device run on T41 .32, daylight): the statistics DMA arrives (about 3200 non-zero words per frame, real zone sums), but `t41_awb_long_prepare` folds the four luminance bins per zone with calibrated bin weights, and on this camera the zones' pixels sit in bin 0 only, while bin 0's weight is the EV-driven Y weight (`p+0xd44 = 1`, table `p+0xd32..` = 0,1,1,2,2,3,4,5,6 over EV knots 3000..600000), which is 0 at daylight EV (about 400). Every zone count ends at 0, the detector falls back (failed 0x3), the gray-world fallback is disabled by the calibration (`p+0xd60/0xd62` = 0), so the published result is unity ratios and CT 5000. Without `t41_tone_trigger=1` the AWB never receives an EV at all (the EV/Y-weight update only runs from the tone worker). Open: whether the luminance bin thresholds (AWB registers 0x18028..0x18038) are what the vendor stack programs; superseded by the EV unit fix above (thresholds match the OEM code; a vendor-driver run on the device stalled after one frame and gave no comparison).
- Read-only diagnostics: `awb_diag` (30 words: detector flags/ratios, DMA non-zero words, global sums, centre zone planes, CT, gains, count gate, bin weights, centre count, Y weight, EV), `awb_diag_cal` (calibration words), `awb_diag_raw` (zone 0 / centre raw statistics, AWB registers 0x18000..0x1804c).

## 2026-10-13: branch `claude/t41-tone-worker` (not merged)

- **T41 per-frame tone worker never ran:** `t41_tmo_workfn` (CCM/BCSH by EV, gamma, LSC gain, ADR, LCE, YSP, CDNS, MDNS, DPC gain, TMO map) is only scheduled from the TMO statistics interrupt (ISR bit 17). On the T41 test camera that bit never fires (new counter `tmo_irq17` stays 0), so on agg-38 `t41_tmo_frames` stayed 0 after 1.5 million frames and every EV/gain-dependent block kept its init state. New module parameter `t41_tone_trigger` (default 0, writable): 1 also schedules the worker from the AE statistics interrupt (bit 4, every frame). Diagnostics: `tmo_irq17`, `tone_kicks`, `tone_gated`, `tmo_live`, `tmo_ready`.
  Device run (T41 .32, module loaded at runtime, with `t41_msca_cfg_update=2`): with 1 the worker runs every frame without errors (`tmo/adr/lce` frames counting, `tmo_error` 0), 30 min under the streamer clean (45 700 worker frames, snapshots 200, no oops). Daylight ch0 numbers change clearly: Y 54 -> 85, detail (mean |dY/dx|) 0.75 -> 1.55, temporal noise 0.4 -> 1.2. Whether that matches the vendor driver is not known yet; stays off by default.
- **T41 AWB colour temperature diagnostics:** read-only `awb_diag_mode/ct_cal/rgain/bgain/frz/change/frz_th`. On the test camera the native AWB publishes CT 5000 and the same gains (520/548) in every frame, also with a valid picture, while the illuminant detection does run (freeze threshold 0): CCM, BCSH and LSC therefore stay on the 5000 K interpolation. Cause not found yet.
- **T41 ModuleControl:** a key that takes DEFOG (bit 11) out of bypass gets -EOPNOTSUPP (the per-frame defog algorithm is not recovered).

- **Quieter kernel log (no functional change):**

## 2026-10-12: aggregate agg-38 (not released)

`claude/agg-38` = agg-37 (e40d7313) + `claude/awbstats` (with `claude/awbzone`), `claude/fcrop-upscale-cap`, `claude/t31-sharpness-fix`, `claude/t41-isp-handlers`, `claude/t41-reload-fix`, `claude/sensor-proc-compat`; `claude/gaps-t23-t31-drv` is recorded as merged without changes (agg-37 has the same patch, with SwitchBin already reworked: validated private copy published by pointer under the bank mutex, no copy over the live banks). Host tests (`make -C tests check`, `tests/t20_fw`) and module builds with the load check (`check_ko_symbols.sh`) for T10/T20/T21/T23/T31/T41; no soak of the aggregate yet.

- **AWB statistics only from a current frame:** T23/T31 `GetAwbZone` with the stock scaling, T41 `GetAwbStatistics` planes; both answer `-EAGAIN` unless the data comes from a statistics frame of the running stream within the last second (device-tested on cam-A, cam-B and cam-F on their branches).
- **T41 reload fixes (`claude/t41-reload-fix`):** VIN index bounded in `csi_core_ops_init` (heap overwrite on reload), the module is pinned while frame-channel files are open, IRQs are freed with their dev_id, `WaitFrameDone` woken from the steady-state frame-done event; 50 load/unload cycles clean on cam-F. Known: `WaitFrameDone` still reports a constant frame counter.

- Further items of this aggregate are listed below under their branches (T41 tuning controls, `/proc/jz/sensor`, T31 sharpness, `fcrop_upscale_pct`).

## 2026-10-12: T41 device run of `claude/t41-isp-handlers` (72e92520, T41 .32 gc5603)

- **Verified on the device:** the `/dev/isp-m0` handle counting (stability branch): a second open/close from another process while the streamer streams leaves brightness/contrast working (Y 33 -> 170 at brightness 200, 3 of 3 rounds), no oops. Reading every `/proc/jz/isp` entry 10 times: clean errors (EIO/EOPNOTSUPP) on the non-text entries, no oops, `proc_watch_hits`/`proc_watch_rb_hits` 0. 20 streamer stop/start cycles without reload: snapshot and RTSP every time, no kernel errors. The IVDC interrupt does not fire with `direct_mode=0` (0 in 60 s).
